# Bytecode nft 1.1.3 (libnftnl 1.2.9) generates for rules Portcullis renders, from

[ lookup reg 1 set monitored_bridges ]
[ meta load iifname => reg 1 ]
[ lookup reg 1 set monitored_ports ]

[ payload load 4b @ network header + 12 => reg 1 ]
[ payload load 4b @ network header + 16 => reg 9 ]
[ meta load l4proto => reg 10 ]
[ payload load 2b @ transport header + 2 => reg 11 ]
[ lookup reg 1 set logged4 0x1 ]
[ limit rate 10/second burst 5 type packets flags 0x0 ]
[ log prefix pc:w:source group 7 snaplen 128 qthreshold 0 ]
